- 2026-11-21 (M12, link embedded-file action, headless): What: `link_embedded_action` (new, headless) reads, sets and removes the go-to-an-embedded-file action of a link: an action dictionary `/S /GoToE` in the link annotation's `/A` (ISO 32000-2, go-to-embedded actions), whose `/T` is a target dictionary (`/R` says whether the file is in a parent (`P`) or a child (`C`) of this document, `/N` is its name in the `/EmbeddedFiles` name tree, `/P` and `/A` name a file attachment annotation, and a nested `/T` is the target of the target), whose `/D` is the place in that file (a name, a string or an array whose first item is an integer page index), and whose optional `/F` (a file specification) and `/NewWindow` put it in another file or ask for a new window. Arguments: `doc`, `page` and `index` (the link, as `link_list` gives them, 1-based), and one of nothing (read), `embedded_file` with `target_page` (and optional `new_window`) or `remove` true. A read reports what is stored, of any kind: `/F` as a string (its text, cut at 255 characters) or as a dictionary (`file_kind` says which; a dictionary's name is not read), `/D` as `destination_kind` (`name`, `string` or `array`) and, for an array that starts with an integer page index of zero or more, `destination_page` (1-based; a negative index or an item that is not an integer reads as none), `/NewWindow` when it is a boolean, the target's `/R` name (`relationship`) and `/N` string (`embedded_file`, cut at 255 characters, `exact` false when the name, the relationship, `/S` or a string `/F` is cut), whether the target has a `/P` or `/A` (`has_annotation_target`) or a nested `/T` (`has_nested_target`) whose stored value is not null (not followed, so a reference counts as present), and whether the action has a `/Next` chain. A set is deliberately narrower than a read: `embedded_file` must be the name of an attachment this document has at that moment (compared byte for byte with the raw keys of the `/EmbeddedFiles` name tree, as read by `attachments::entries`, which is how a viewer looks a name up: a key stored as UTF-16 reads as the same text in `doc_info` but is not found by an ASCII name, and a set naming it is refused) and be 1 to 255 bytes of letters, digits, `.`, `_`, `-`, space and parentheses (the name `add_attachment` gives a second file of one name, `name (2)`), with no space at either end, no leading `-` and at least one letter or digit, and `target_page` is 1 to 1,000,000; it writes `/T << /R /C /N (name) >>` and `/D [page-1 /Fit]` and nothing else, so no `/F`, no `/P`, no `/A`, no nested `/T`, no named destination and no target in a parent document. A set replaces the link's whole `/A` with a new direct dictionary (`/Type /Action`, `/S /GoToE`, `/D`, `/T`, and `/NewWindow` only when given) and takes `/Dest` out, which a link with an action must not have; a `/Next` chain, an `/F` or any other entry of the old action is not kept, and an indirect old `/A` dictionary is left in the file unreferenced; the result's `replaced` says what was there (`action`, `has_entry`, `destination`, `next`). A removal takes `/A` out, with any `/Next` chain, only when it is a GoToE action; a link with no action, or another kind of action (a launch or a remote go-to included), is `changed` false: nothing is written and no undo step is added. A set that repeats what the link has is written again, as one more undo step. The tool refuses, before anything is written: `remove` with `embedded_file`, `target_page` or `new_window`, `new_window` without both `embedded_file` and `target_page`, one of them without the other, a name or page that fails the rules above, a name that is not an attachment of the document, a link that does not exist or is written in the page and has no object, and an object that is not a link annotation or is the catalog, the `/Pages` node, a node under it or a page (compared by object number). A write is one undo step (`Edit::SetLinkEmbeddedAction`, labelled Set link embedded-file action, now in `EDIT_LABELS`) that needs permission to modify the document. It does not check that the attachment is a PDF file or has that many pages, does not check for PDF/A (which limits embedded files and some actions), and does not run the action. Sibling tools that exist already: `link_launch_action`, `link_named_action` and `link_thread_action` do the same for the other three actions, `link_add` and `link_edit` write web, page and remote-document links, and `link_list` reports a link with a GoToE action only as `other: "GoToE"`; the optimizer's Discard external cross-references already removes these actions from a copy. Engine: `pdfcraft_organize::embedded_action` (`read_embedded_action`, `embedded_action_change`, `set_embedded_action`, `EmbeddedTarget`, `MAX_NAME_BYTES`, `MAX_PAGE`), run by the new edit, with `Document::link_embedded_action` and `Document::link_embedded_action_change`. `edit.action-goto-embedded` (Go to a target in an embedded file (GoToE)) moves from planned to partial; the GoToE row of `docs/pdf-spec-parity.md` now says what is there. Why: `edit.action-goto-embedded` was planned with nothing behind it, and the GoToE row of the spec table said none. A headless agent could not give a link an action that goes to a page of a file attached to the document, or see that a link had one. The action has a small definition in the standard and can be tested in a saved file by what the link's `/A` holds. It is read, set and remove only, with a deliberately narrow set (a page of an existing attachment of this document), no viewer that follows it, no UI and no other target, so the entry is partial. Cost: each call of `read_embedded_action` and `embedded_action_change` has one allowance of 10,000 reads (`MAX_GETS`), charged before each `Document::get` is made, and stops with `capped` (a read) or a refusal that names the allowance (a change) when it cannot pay: one for the link and up to 33 for each reference chain behind `/A`, `/NewWindow`, `/F`, `/D`, the first item of a `/D` array, `/T`, and the `/R` and `/N` of `/T` (as `Document::resolve` follows them, the first object and up to 32 more, each hop charged), so at most 1 + 8 * 33 = 265 reads, far below the allowance, which is a second guard and is tested only with small allowances (`read_with`, `change_with`); a change check needs nothing more than that read. `set_embedded_action` runs a change check and then writes. Separate from that and not charged by this module: the page-tree walk that decides whether the object is a structural one is `reference_xobjects::structural`, which has its own allowance (`reference_xobjects::MAX_GETS`), charged before each read, and whose first read, of the catalog, is not charged; it runs once for each read of an object that is a link: once for a read and 4 times for a set through the tool (its check, the extra read before the edit, the applied edit and the answer); the listing of the raw attachment keys (`attachments::entries`, the `/Names /EmbeddedFiles` name tree under the limits of `crate::import::name_tree_entries`) runs once for each change check of a set (a removal makes none), is not charged against the allowance, and for a tree past its limits lists only what was read, so a set can refuse a name that is there; and the tool finds the link with `Document::links`, which reads the annotations of every page under the limits of `crates/annot/src/links.rs`. A name is kept cut at 127 input bytes (`/S`, `/R`) or 255 characters (`/N`, `/F`). No stream is decoded. No new dependency, asset or workspace crate, and no change to a saved file that does not call the tool. One new module (`crates/organize/src/embedded_action.rs`, 362 lines), one new edit and one new tool, 421 added lines in `crates/organize/src/tests.rs` (seven tests) and 290 in `crates/automation/tests/it/link_embedded_action.rs` (four tests). Tested: in `crates/organize/src/tests.rs`: `a_go_to_embedded_action_is_read_with_its_target_and_destination` (a string `/F` with `/D [2 /Fit]`, `/NewWindow`, `/T` with `/R /C` and `/N`, and a `/Next` chain; a parent target with `/P`, `/A` and a nested `/T` stored, and a name and a string `/D`; only `/A`, only `/P`, and null `/P`, `/A` and `/T` reading as none; a first item of `/D` that is a page object, `-1`, `i64::MIN` or a name, an empty array and `i64::MAX` (no overflow) all reading no page; a file specification dictionary reported as one and not read, an `/F` and a `/T` that are numbers, an `/R` and an `/N` of the wrong type and a `/NewWindow` that is a string reading as none; the file, destination, target, relationship and name behind references; a 300-byte name cut at 255 and not exact; the names `GoToR`, `GoToEx`, `GoTo` and `Launch` reading as other actions with no target; a link with only a `/Dest`; null `/A` and `/Dest`; a widget, a number and a missing object reading as not a link), `a_go_to_embedded_action_is_set_replaced_removed_and_survives_a_save` (a set over an indirect web action and a `/Dest` writes a direct `/Action` dictionary with `/S /GoToE`, `/D [2 /Fit]` for page 3, a `/T` of exactly `/R /C` and `/N`, `/NewWindow`, no `/F` and no `/Next`, takes `/Dest` out, leaves the old object and the rest of the link, and reads back after a full save; a set without a window writes none and drops an old `/Next`, `/F`, `/P` target and window; a removal takes only `/A` out; a second removal is `NoChange` and the saved bytes are equal; a web action, a remote go-to and a launch are `NoChange` for a removal and kept; a set naming no attachment is refused and the saved bytes are equal), `an_embedded_target_is_a_plain_name_and_a_page` (ten accepted names, among them the longest allowed, one made only of a digit, ones with `.`, `_`, `-` and two spaces inside, and two with parentheses, and 28 refused ones: empty, a space at either end, a leading `-`, only dots and spaces, `-` alone, `/`, a backslash, `:`, `;`, a backtick, `$(`, a vertical bar, `&`, both quotes, `*`, `?`, `<` and `>`, `%`, `~`, non-ASCII, a newline, a NUL, a backslash with a parenthesis and 256 bytes; pages 0, 1,000,001 and `u32::MAX` refused and 1,000,000 accepted), `a_go_to_embedded_set_names_an_attachment_by_its_raw_key_and_takes_the_name_of_a_second_file` (a second file of one name, `dup.pdf (2)`, is accepted, written and read back whole after a save; a key stored as UTF-16 reads as `inner.pdf` in `attachment_keys` and a set naming it is refused, with a refusal from `set_embedded_action` too), `a_go_to_embedded_action_is_refused_on_what_is_not_a_link` (the catalog, the `/Pages` node, an inner node, a page, a widget, a number and a missing object refused for a set and a removal with the saved bytes equal; a page marked as a link still refused, the reason containing the word page), `every_read_a_go_to_embedded_action_makes_is_paid_for_before_it_is_made` (a direct action with window, file, destination and target is read with exactly 1 read and with 0 not even the link; an action, a window, a file, a destination, its first item, a target, a relationship and a name behind references need 9 reads in all and with each one fewer the next field in that order is not read) and `a_chain_of_references_behind_a_go_to_embedded_action_is_paid_for_hop_by_hop` (chains of 32 references are followed with 1 + 33 reads and one of 33 is not; the same for a target's name and for the first item of `/D`; a change check is allowed with 1 read and refused naming the allowance with 0, and with a reference to the action it needs 2); and in `crates/automation/tests/it/link_embedded_action.rs`, through `link_embedded_action`, on a document that has an attachment: `a_go_to_embedded_action_is_read_set_saved_removed_undone_and_redone` (a read of a web link and of a GoToE with an `/F`, a parent target and a `/Next`; sets found in the saved file as `/S /GoToE` with the name, `/R /C`, the zero-based page and the window; `link_list` reporting `other` GoToE; a `/Dest`, an `/F` and a `/Next` going; a removal; a second removal `changed` false with equal bytes; five undo steps labelled Set link embedded-file action, the saved bytes after the last equal to the file before the first set, and five redos equal to the file saved before the undos; the clock is pinned), `a_removal_leaves_another_kind_of_action_and_a_set_needs_its_attachment` (a removal on a web link is `changed` false; a name that is not an attachment is refused naming the missing attachment, with the saved bytes equal and no undo step), `a_call_that_cannot_be_applied_is_named_and_changes_nothing` (twenty-two refusals by message, among them a `;`, a `/` and a leading `-` in `embedded_file`, page 0, -1 and 1,000,001, each half of the pair alone, and `new_window` alone; saved bytes equal and no undo step) and `a_modify_restriction_refuses_a_write_and_still_reads` (a copy protected against changes refuses a set, still reads, and a removal of nothing is `changed` false). None of them could fail before the change, because the module and the tool did not exist. The hostile-argument sweep in `crates/automation/tests/it/hostile_args.rs` runs over every tool in the table, including this one. Mutations: 9 run on the final code (no code line was edited after them; after them only the parity notes, the docs, ROADMAP and this text changed), and all 9 failed a test: in the organize module, against the eight organize tests whose names contain `embedded` (seven of them new), the attachment check compared as decoded text, the attachment check dropped, parentheses taken out of the allowlist, a semicolon admitted, the leading `-` refusal dropped, the letter-or-digit rule dropped, a trailing space admitted, the 255-byte limit dropped and page 0 admitted. The other mutations of the first run (28 on the code before this amendment; 24 in the organize module and 4 in the tool) were not run again; they touch lines this amendment did not edit, except the attachment check and the allowlist, which the 9 cover. Those were: `/S` always read as GoToE, `/Dest` not taken out by a set, a removal of a link with no GoToE action counted as a change, the page written without the shift to zero-based, `/R` written as `P`, `/NewWindow` never written, the page limit one too high, the first item of `/D` read without following a reference, a negative page index kept, the relationship not read, the target's `/A` ignored for `has_annotation_target`, the nested target ignored, a string `/F` not read, `exact` not cleared for a cut name, a dictionary `/F` not reported, a string `/D` not reported and the `/T` not written; and in the tool, `replaced` never reported, `new_window` alone accepted, a negative `target_page` cast to an unsigned number and `embedded_file` without `target_page` accepted with page 1. Survived: none. Not mutated: the allowance of 10,000 reads (tested only through `read_with` and `change_with` with small ones), the 33 reads of a reference chain (`Gets::follow` in `crates/organize/src/reference_xobjects.rs`, shared and covered by the chain tests here), the walk of the page tree cut short (`!cut_short` in `settable`), the clearing of `exact` for a cut string `/F`, the tool's result fields other than `replaced`, `Document::link_embedded_action` and `Document::link_embedded_action_change` when a document has no editor, the `EDIT_LABELS` entry (its text is asserted by the undo test but was not mutated), and the re-exports in `crates/engine/src/lib.rs`. Not tested: a set on a PDF/A file (nothing checks it), a set that repeats what the link has (it writes again and adds an undo step), an attachment that is not a PDF file or has fewer pages than `target_page` (a set does not check), an attachment name tree past the limits of `name_tree_entries` (a set can refuse a name that is there), two attachments of one name, an indirect `/S` (read as no action, not followed), a stored name that is not valid UTF-8 or is UTF-16 text (read through `text_chars`, which this change does not test), a link object that is itself a reference to another object (it reads as not a link), an `/A` or `/Annots` array that is a stream or behind a reference chain on a page, a link shared by two pages, a page-tree walk over its limits, an action written by this tool followed or read by another viewer, and the optimizer's removal of an action this tool wrote. Ran: `cargo fmt --check`; `cargo clippy -p pdfcraft-organize -p pdfcraft-engine -p pdfcraft-automation --all-targets -- -D warnings` (without any `-A` allowance, exit 0); `cargo test --no-fail-fast` on each of the three crates in turn (organize: 373 library tests passed and 2 in other targets passed; engine: 894 library tests passed (2 ignored) and 72 in other targets passed (2 ignored); automation: 80 library tests passed, 694 integration tests passed and 1 failed, and 2 in another target passed; 0 doctests in each); `cargo xtask layers`; `cargo xtask assets`; `cargo xtask parity`; `cargo xtask wasm`; `cargo xtask i18n-report` (it skipped: no craft-i18n checkout); the 9 mutations above. All of these ran on the final code after the last edit to a code line. Failed, not related: the known root-only `batch_failures::read_only_output_folder_publishes_nothing` (fails when run as root). Not run: ui-egui tests and the applications' tests (not touched: the app does not use the new engine functions; `cargo xtask wasm` compiled the ui-egui library with the new edit), the optimize crate's tests (it already removes GoToE actions; not touched), the translations of the new undo label (i18n-report had no craft-i18n checkout to read), the workspace sweep, the new tests on Windows or macOS (only Linux was run), a viewer other than PdfCraft reading an action this tool wrote, and the mutations listed under Not mutated. Estimates unchanged.
- 2026-11-21 (M7, link file action, headless): What: `link_launch_action` (new, headless) reads, sets and removes the open-a-file action of a link: an action dictionary `/S /Launch` in the link annotation's `/A` (ISO 32000-2 12.6.4.6, Table 207), whose `/F` is a file specification and whose optional `/NewWindow` asks for a new window. Arguments: `doc`, `page` and `index` (the link, as `link_list` gives them, 1-based), and one of nothing (read), `file` (with optional `new_window`) or `remove` true. A read reports what is stored, of any kind: `/F` as a string or as a file specification dictionary (its `/UF`, else its `/F`, as text, cut at 255 characters; `file_kind` says which; `url` is true when the dictionary's `/FS` is `URL`), `/NewWindow` when it is a boolean, and whether the action has a `/Next` chain or a `/Win`, `/Mac` or `/Unix` entry whose value is not null (`platform`; a key whose value is null counts as none here, while the render crate's `launch_pdf` counts a `/Win` key even when its value is null, and then does not read the launch as a link). A set is deliberately narrower than a read: `file` must be a relative path to a `.pdf` file (only letters, digits, `.`, `_`, `-`, space and `/`, 1 to 255 bytes, so no backslash, no colon, no drive, no URL and no shell character; no leading `/` and no space at either end; no part between slashes that is empty, made only of dots or spaces (so no `.` or `..`) or starting with `-`), because a document written by this tool must not name a program or leave its folder; the render crate already reads a launch of a `.pdf` file without `/Win` as a link to that file (`launch_pdf` in `crates/render/src/inspect/annotations.rs`) and no other launch, and a set writes only what that reads. A set replaces the link's whole `/A` with a new direct dictionary (`/Type /Action`, `/S /Launch`, `/F` a string, and `/NewWindow` only when given) and takes `/Dest` out, which a link with an action must not have; a `/Next` chain, platform entries or any other entry of the old action are not kept, and an indirect old `/A` dictionary is left in the file unreferenced; the result's `replaced` says what was there (`action`, `has_entry`, `destination`, `next`, `platform`). A removal takes `/A` out only when it is a launch action, whatever it launches (including a program); a link with no action, or another kind of action, is `changed` false: nothing is written and no undo step is added. A set that repeats what the link has is written again, as one more undo step. The tool refuses, before anything is written: `remove` with `file` or with `new_window`, `new_window` without `file`, a `file` that fails the rules above, a link that does not exist or is written in the page and has no object, and an object that is not a link annotation or is the catalog, the `/Pages` node, a node under it or a page (compared by object number). A write is one undo step (`Edit::SetLinkLaunchAction`, labelled Set link file action, now in `EDIT_LABELS`) that needs permission to modify the document. Sibling tools that exist already: `link_named_action` and `link_thread_action` do the same for the other two actions, `link_add` and `link_edit` write web, page and remote-document links, and `link_list` reports a link with a launch action only as `other: "Launch"`. PDF/A does not allow launch actions and a set does not check for that. It does not run the action, and writes no `/Win`, `/Mac` or `/Unix` entry, no file specification dictionary and no non-PDF target. Engine: `pdfcraft_organize::launch_action` (`read_launch_action`, `launch_action_change`, `set_launch_action`, `LaunchTarget`, `MAX_FILE_BYTES`), run by the new edit, with `Document::link_launch_action` and `Document::link_launch_action_change`. `edit.action-launch` (Open-a-file action) moves from planned to partial; the Launch row of `docs/pdf-spec-parity.md` now says what is there. Why: `edit.action-launch` was planned with nothing behind it, although the render crate already read a launch of a PDF file. A headless agent could not give a link an open-a-file action or see that a link had one. The action has a small definition in the standard and can be tested in a saved file by what the link's `/A` holds. It is read, set and remove only, with a deliberately narrow set (PDF targets only), no UI and no other target, so the entry is partial. Cost: each call of `read_launch_action` and `launch_action_change` has one allowance of 10,000 reads (`MAX_GETS`), charged before each `Document::get` is made, and stops with `capped` (a read) or a refusal that names the allowance (a change) when it cannot pay: one for the link and up to 33 for each reference chain behind `/A`, `/NewWindow`, `/F` and, when `/F` is a dictionary, behind its `/UF`, `/F` and `/FS` (as `Document::resolve` follows them, the first object and up to 32 more, each hop charged; the `/F` of the dictionary is not read when its `/UF` is a string), so at most 1 + 6 * 33 = 199 reads, far below the allowance, which is a second guard and is tested only with small allowances (`read_with`, `change_with`); a change check needs nothing more than that read. `set_launch_action` runs a change check and then writes. Separate from that, whether the object is a structural one is decided by the page-tree walk of `reference_xobjects::structural`, which has its own allowance (`reference_xobjects::MAX_GETS`), charged before each read, and whose first read, of the catalog, is not charged; it runs once for each read of an object that is a link, and the tool makes it as many times as `link_named_action` does (once for a read, and for each check, applied edit and answer of a write). Not charged by this module: the tool finds the link with `Document::links`, which reads the annotations of every page under the limits of `crates/annot/src/links.rs`. A name or a path is kept cut at 127 input bytes (`/S`) or 255 characters (a path). No stream is decoded. No new dependency, asset or workspace crate, and no change to a saved file that does not call the tool. One new module (`crates/organize/src/launch_action.rs`, 309 lines), one new edit and one new tool, 318 added lines in `crates/organize/src/tests.rs` (five tests) and 216 in `crates/automation/tests/it/link_launch_action.rs` (three tests). the names `Launc` and `Launchx` read as other actions; a widget, a number and a missing object reading as not a link), `a_launch_action_is_set_replaced_removed_and_survives_a_save` (a set over an indirect web action and a `/Dest` writes a direct `/Action` dictionary with `/S /Launch`, `/F` and `/NewWindow`, takes `/Dest` out, leaves the old object and the rest of the link, and reads back after a full save; a set without a window writes none and drops an old `/Next`, `/Win` and window; a removal takes only `/A` out; a second removal is `NoChange` and the saved bytes are equal; a launch of `run.exe` is removed; a web action is `NoChange` for a removal and kept), `a_launch_target_is_a_relative_path_to_a_pdf_file_and_nothing_else` (eight accepted paths, among them the longest allowed and ones with `_`, `-`, a space and dots inside a part, and 41 refused ones: empty, `.pdf`, other extensions, absolute, drive, URL, `file:`, backslashes, `..` and `.` and `...` and space-only parts, an empty part, spaces at the ends, a trailing `/`, a leading `-` in the name and in a folder, a backtick, `$(`, `;`, `|`, `&`, both quotes, `*`, `?`, `<` and `>`, `%`, `~`, non-ASCII, a newline, a NUL and 256 bytes), `a_launch_action_is_refused_on_what_is_not_a_link` (the catalog, the `/Pages` node, an inner node, a page, a widget, a number and a missing object refused for a set and a removal with the saved bytes equal; and in `crates/automation/tests/it/link_launch_action.rs`, through `link_launch_action`: `a_launch_action_is_read_set_saved_removed_undone_and_redone` (a read of a web link and of a launch with a window, a `/Win` and a `/Next`; sets found in the saved file as `/S /Launch` with the file and window; `link_list` reporting `other` Launch; a `/Dest` and a `/Next` plus `/Win` going; a removal; a second removal `changed` false with equal bytes; five undo steps labelled Set link file action, the saved bytes after the last equal to the file before the first set, and five redos equal to the file saved before the undos; the clock is pinned), `a_call_that_cannot_be_applied_is_named_and_changes_nothing` (seventeen refusals by message, among them a `;` and a leading `-` in `file`; saved bytes equal and no undo step) and `a_modify_restriction_refuses_a_write_and_still_reads` (a copy protected against changes refuses a set, still reads, and a removal of nothing is `changed` false).
