- **2026-10-28 (M2, PR #4172):** Integrated main 5af10276, preserving both roadmap histories and reviewing auto-merged export tests and accessibility UI changes. Validation: 488 scoped tests passed (18 engine, 412 renderer unit, 43 renderer integration, 15 Combine; one existing renderer integration test ignored); scoped all-target Clippy with -D warnings, formatting, layers, assets, parity and renderer/engine WASM checks passed. Viewer-only CJK fallback and print/output font policy remain unchanged; estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-27 (M2, PR #4172 module-split conflicts):** Integrated main 1dd2737b, retaining viewer-only CJK fallback in split session modules and Combine thumbnail workers, and moving renderer regressions to the new test module. Validation: 798 scoped tests passed (371 engine, 412 renderer, 15 Combine; two existing engine tests ignored); scoped all-target Clippy with -D warnings, formatting, layers, assets, parity and renderer/engine WASM checks passed. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-27 (M10, `ocr.ocr-web`, rebase fix):** The web build loads OCR models only when their SHA-256 matches `ATTRIBUTION.toml`. With ONNX loading now on, rten's parser spins forever on some bytes that aren't a model (a corrupt or wrong download), so a bad file now fails at once instead of hanging a worker.
- **2026-10-27 (M3, #3425):** A file named with open parameters, as help systems start a PDF reader (`manual.pdf#nameddest=Intro`, `#page=12` or `#Intro`), opens at that destination instead of failing to read `manual.pdf#…`: at launch, when another launch hands it over, and in its tab when it is already open. Other parameters are ignored. The app and `link_open` now also apply a GoToR link's named destination, looked up in the target's /Names /Dests or catalog /Dests, instead of warning that it wasn't applied; the app looks names up off the UI thread. `doc_open` reports the destination of a path with open parameters. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-27 (M10, OCR models):** Model staging downloads and verifies the ONNX pair after the upstream RTEN URL brownout; runtime discovery prefers complete ONNX pairs per directory, retains complete RTEN fallback and preserves directory precedence.

- **2026-10-26 (M2, PR #4172):** Reconciled current main, preserving its Japanese serif/weight selection and both branches' progress records. Installed CJK viewer and preview fallback remains display-only; estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-29 (M7, #2517):** Widening a paragraph rejoins a wrap with the number of spaces that wrap consumed, including a typed run and none for a Chinese or Japanese word split. The count survives an incremental save and a full rewrite, and a width-only `text_edit` uses it. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-29 (M2):** Scanned pages look like the paper again. An image that doesn't ask for /Interpolate was shrunk by nearest-neighbour (since #635), which kept one sample in N per axis, so the text of a 300 dpi scan at 100% lost most of its strokes; it is now area-averaged to whole device pixels (on a 13-page 300 dpi scan every sample at 96–192 dpi is within 1 of the exact area average; it was up to 255 off). Enlarged, an image of page-scan size is smoothed bilinearly, so a zoomed scan has no staircases; icons, QR codes and pixel art keep hard pixels. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-26 (M11, JBIG2 symbols):** Lossless JBIG2 now stores each distinct mark of a black-and-white image once, as a symbol, and places its exact copies, when that is smaller than the generic region: on six pages of rendered 300 dpi text 28% of the generic region's size and 17% of Group 4's, and the same bits back through two independent decoders (hayro-jbig2 and Poppler). Scans, whose letters are seldom identical, gain little until refinement coding; a dictionary shared between pages (`/JBIG2Globals`) is next. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-26 (M3, view.text-select):** Text selection runs across pages: dragging onto another page (also a quick flick off the page, and the edge and wheel scrolling of #721/#1670) or ⇧-clicking there selects the rest of the first page, the pages between and the last page up to the pointer. Copy has each page's part on a line of its own, reading pages not yet read off the UI thread; highlight, underline, strikethrough and Redact put one mark on each page in one undo step (a tool that finishes first marks once the pages are read; Redact refuses a selection over a page whose text could not be read); Add comment and Replace text stay one-page. A drag from one page's text that is already over the next when it starts no longer begins a Redact or area-highlight box there. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-25 (M5, comment replies):** One reply can be deleted from the Comments panel without its thread. On the selected card each reply has its own "…" menu (Edit, Copy text, Delete); Delete takes that reply with its pop-up and the replies to it, as `comment_delete` already did headlessly, and the comment and its other replies stay. The card stays selected, and the panel's editor stays open, on what they were on though the delete moved it in `/Annots`. Undo and Redo close the editor, since either can move what it was open on. A locked reply can't be deleted. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-25 (M7, #2750):** Single-style Keep source fonts edits retain source glyphs and batch only missing glyphs in fallback fonts. Per-rewrite character eligibility and widths are cached; source and fallback edge spaces stay extractable. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-25 (M10, #4399):** The printer picker has a wider searchable list, wrapped device names, stable selection while filtering, and Search and Save as PDF kept visible. Synthetic fleet UI regressions cover long lists and a smaller window. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-25 (web, same-document host mode):** A page that loads the PdfCraft web module into its own document (no iframe) can opt into the host bridge with `data-host="page"` on the app's canvas — the exact value `page`; any other value keeps the normal app with downloads, unless a same-origin framed `?host=parent` selects the parent bridge (unframed or cross-origin, `?host=parent` also keeps the normal app). It is the `?host=parent` bridge with the transport swapped: the same five messages travel as non-bubbling `CustomEvent`s on the canvas (`detail` holds the parent messages' fields without `type`; the host answers with a `pdfcraft:open` event), so saves reach the host instead of downloading and dirty and start-up-failure reporting are unchanged. Unit tests cover the opt-in rule; no parity or estimate changes.
- **2026-10-25 (M10):** Vector printing keeps field and comment artwork whose rectangle coordinates are indirect, requiring exactly four finite resolved values.
- **2026-10-26 (M10):** Vector printing follows up to 64 indirect references for annotation rectangles, coordinates and appearance-state names, rejecting cycles and longer chains without changing source objects.
- **2026-10-25 (M10):** Vector printing keeps field and comment artwork whose rectangle coordinates or appearance-state names are indirect, requiring exactly four finite resolved rectangle values and a usable state name for state dictionaries.
  Synthetic CLI and source regressions cover the behavior.
  Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-24 (M10, #4158 partial):** Word, HTML, RTF and PowerPoint export keep a block that continues another on its line (an italic case name, a link, a superscript) as a run in its own style inside that paragraph, with the source's word space, instead of a paragraph of its own. Style changes that wrap onto the next line still split paragraphs (the reading blocks keep style boundaries). Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-24 (M3, window move on Wayland, #3970 follow-up):** After a move or resize by PdfCraft's own title bar or edges, the button release given back to egui goes before the pointer leaving the window, so hover no longer sticks at the old place until the pointer moves. Found by CodeRabbit in review of #3970; a unit test covers it. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-26 (M10, `ocr.ocr-web`, review fix):** On the web, Edit on a scanned page starts the OCR model download, and the page is read once the models load; it was taken for no scan and left unread until the document changed.
- **2026-10-24 (M10, `ocr.ocr-web`, review fix):** On the web, single-file and multiple-file OCR running at once no longer lose a page: the workers' answers go to the run that sent each page, instead of to whichever run collected first, and a cancelled run's late answers are let go. Regression tests cover both start orders and cancellation. Files chosen for Recognize Text in Multiple Files before the models have downloaded now wait for them instead of having to be chosen again, and idle workers close on time even when nothing is happening on the page, a full minute after their last page comes back rather than after it was handed out (checked by hand in Firefox); estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-24 (M14, #4253):** The annotation quick bar remembers Keep tool selected across restarts, including unpinning. Old and malformed settings keep safe defaults; the opt-in UI control channel reads and changes the same preference. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-22 (M7, #2517):** A partial rewrite of the last paragraph closes its marker after that paragraph's last `ET`, so a row left in the source stays in the paragraph. When the next paragraph shares the text object, the marker closes before that neighbour instead of after the shared `ET`. The extra marker is not added across two marked-content regions. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M7, #2517):** A blank line between paragraphs stays a paragraph break. An explicit `TL` is the measured baseline gap. Without one, three or more rows at a repeated step stay one paragraph, and a single wider gap stays a break. Adding a row, even when an earlier row is left in the source, does not absorb the next paragraph on a later edit. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-24 (M11, more black-and-white images):** The optimizer re-stores Group 3 fax images as Group 4 when that is smaller, instead of leaving any CCITT image it doesn't resample as it is. Black-and-white images stored as eight-bit RGB (as some phone and scanner apps write them) become one-bit grey, and indexed images that use only their first two palette entries become one bit with their palette, so Monochrome Images compresses and downsamples them too.
- **2026-10-24 (M11, monochrome downsampling):** The PDF Optimizer and Reduce File Size average black-and-white images above 450 ppi down to 300 ppi (Monochrome Images ▸ Downsample to), Group 4 scans included, so a 600 ppi text page goes from 79 KB to 40 KB. CCITT decoding moved into `pdfcraft-filters` (`decode_ccitt`), shared by Export ▸ Images and the optimizer through `Document::decoded_image`.
- **2026-10-24 (M11, black-and-white pictures):** A scan saved as a 1-bit PNG became an eight-bit grey image when made into a PDF, larger than the PNG; Create PDF now stores pictures of only black and white at one bit (Group 4 when smaller), the optimizer packs eight-bit black-and-white grey images to one bit unless the grey settings make them smaller, as they can a dithered scan, soft mask counted, and Export ▸ Images reads CCITT fax images.
- **2026-10-23 (M11, JBIG2):** The PDF Optimizer can store black-and-white images as lossless JBIG2 (a generic region, written from the JBIG2 draft standard and checked against its arithmetic-coder test sequence), about a third smaller than Group 4 on 300 dpi text, and Export ▸ Images reads JBIG2 images. JBIG2 symbol coding, where its largest gains on text are, is next.
- **2026-10-23 (M11, monochrome compression):** The PDF Optimizer and Reduce File Size store black-and-white images (scans, image masks) losslessly as CCITT Group 4 when that is smaller, with a Monochrome Images setting in the Optimizer and `doc_optimize` `mono`. JBIG2 and monochrome downsampling are next.
- **2026-10-23 (M6, calculation checks):** The calculation order now flags a formula that reads a field the form doesn't have (a field deleted from under a sum, a typo in notation, a script's `getField`) and a script that builds the names it reads at run time. Prepare a form ▸ Calculation order marks both on the row and in a card that names the broken formulas, in place of its all-clear. `form_calculation_order` gives each field's formula, and `form_fields` shows a custom Calculate script.
- **2026-10-23 (M4, #3923):** A recovered document's original path is only a suggestion: the first Save after recovering opens Save As at that path instead of writing it, since anyone who can write the recovery folder (a shared portable `PdfCraftData`, for example) can name any file in a sidecar. The file chosen is the document's file from then on. Sidecars are read through a 64 KiB cap; a longer one is not read, and its snapshot recovers without metadata. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-23 (M7, #4280):** Edit text names each standard stand-in font after its face (`PCEdTimesBold`, ...) instead of one shared `PCEdHelv`. Rewriting a bold paragraph whose subset lacks an accent no longer repoints the paragraphs set earlier in a regular stand-in at Times-Bold, and the editor's frame stays on the paragraph being edited. Pages saved by older builds keep their shared name and are not repaired. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-23 (M8, #3205 follow-up):** Phone-pattern redaction matches complete labelled Italian geographic numbers such as `Tel. +39 0426/123456`, with synthetic matcher and mark/apply/save/reopen coverage; unlabelled international slash numbers remain unsupported.
- **2026-10-23 (M7, #2636 partial):** A new `PasteContent` edit pastes an added text box or image copied in the same document or another, keeping its style, picture, turns, flips and crop, as one undo step; `content_copy` drives it headlessly. A paste lands beside earlier copies (18 pt steps) and stays on the page. A pasted image reuses its picture object in its own document and after the first paste into another; there a colour profile is dropped and CMYK colours stored losslessly are turned to RGB. The desktop keys and menus follow in separate PRs. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-23 (M11, optimizer resolution):** Image downsampling includes each page's `/UserUnit` scale, preserving images already below the physical resolution threshold, including shared nested forms.
  Synthetic measurement and optimization regressions cover direct and indirect units and page-local defaults.
  Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-23 (M10, Word table widths):** Word export sizes each cell from the grid columns it spans, keeping unequal columns and cells after a merged cell consistent with the table grid.
- **2026-10-23 (M10, table continuation text):** Office exporters retain text from table slots covered by another cell's span as paragraph text.
  Synthetic regressions check every input fragment survives exactly once in Word, HTML, RTF and PowerPoint, while ordinary superscripts stay in their table cells.
- **2026-10-23 (M10, image export):** Soft-mask Decode intervals preserve partial opacity in Export all images and Save Image As.
  Synthetic regressions cover direct and indirect ranges, inverted/subrange alpha, packed masks, JPEG masks and unchanged image component Decode.
- **2026-10-23 (M10, print resources):** Vector printing preserves original page artwork when existing XObjects use annotation resource names.
  The regression checks two distinct appearances, inherited resources, print/reopen pixels and unchanged source data.
  Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-21 (M3, window resize and move on Wayland):** With PdfCraft's own title bar on GNOME/Wayland, a second resize by a window edge, or a second move by the empty tab strip, works straight after the first; it waited for a click inside the window, because the compositor keeps the button's release from egui. A unit test covers the release given back; checked by hand in a test build. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-22 (M6, #4088):** Automatic AcroForm Keystroke, Validate, Calculate and Format scripts cannot open the print dialog; ignored requests are reported in the JavaScript console. Explicit button and console printing remain available, including when they trigger automatic calculations. Engine and headless automation regressions cover the boundary. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-24 (M5, move preview):** A comment being moved is drawn where it goes while it is dragged (a rectangle or oval showed only its blue frame): the comment is rendered alone once as the drag starts and laid over the page, which shows it where it was until the move is applied. Engine `Document::comment_layer`; synthetic engine and UI regressions. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-22 (M10, #3132):** Windows printing no longer swaps the letters of embedded TrueType subsets. A subset renumbers its glyphs (ReportLab's Arial space is glyph 11, the installed Arial's is 3), and the Windows print path drew those numbers with the installed font of the same name: `Revisión` printed as `Wj{nxn`, the bold `z` as `Ä`, on Brother queues and the Adobe PDF printer alike. The copy sent to the Windows spooler now names every embedded TrueType/OpenType program `PdfCraftPrint<n>`, in its `name` table and in the PDF; glyphs, encodings and `/ToUnicode` are untouched and a synthetic test renders the page identically before and after. Not yet checked on a physical Windows printer. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-24 (M5, border shape):** Text boxes and Fill & Sign's typed text take an oval or a circle for their border (Comment Properties ▸ Border shape, Fill & Sign's toolbar): the text box keeps its place and the shape goes around it, the text centred, a circle snug around the text (its box no wider than the text needs); stored as /RD and /PCBorderShape and drawn in the appearance. comment_add / comment_edit / fill_sign_add `border_shape`. One new label in every catalog. Synthetic annot, UI and automation regressions. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-24 (M5, Escape in Fill & Sign):** Escape leaves a Fill & Sign tool as it leaves a comment tool: the first press closes the open type box, the next goes back to Select, so the Text tool no longer opens a box at every click with no way out by the keyboard. A synthetic UI regression covers the Text and Checkmark tools. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-23 (M5, ortho):** Shift while drawing holds a line or arrow level or plumb, whichever is nearer (a CAD drawing's ortho mode), and each new side of connected lines, a polygon or a cloud from the corner before; dragging an end or corner with Shift is held the same way (it turned to 45° steps). `measure::snap::ortho`; `comment_add ortho`. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-24 (M3, arrow keys on a wide page):** → / ← scroll a page wider than the window (a plan zoomed in) sideways a line at a time and turn the page at its edge, as ↓ / ↑ do in single-page view; ⌘→ / ⌘← turn it at once, and a page that fits across turns as before (#185). A synthetic viewer regression covers both. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-24 (M5, part opacity):** Comment Properties sets a text box's text, border and fill, and a shape's line and fill, each at an opacity of its own (a see-through fill with an opaque line on a plan); stored as /CA, /ca (PDF 2.0) and /PCTextOpacity and drawn with separate graphics states, so other viewers show it. Make Properties Default and the settings carry it; comment_add / comment_edit `fill_opacity`, `text_opacity`. Four new labels in every catalog. Synthetic annot, UI and automation regressions. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-22 (M4, #3916 partial):** Document edits and Undo/Redo cancel an Organize page drag. Pointer-release regressions check exact page order and save/reopen.
- **2026-10-22 (M10, #3752 paper):** CUPS jobs ask for the chosen paper in every size. The 13 sizes the Print dialog sent no paper for (A6, A0–A2, ARCH, ANSI C–E) use PWG 5101.1 names, checked against a synthetic PPD with `cupsfilter`, and `doc_print` to a printer sends the paper too. The macOS tray half (Auto Select instead of the PPD's default tray) is a craft-print change that needs a pin bump here. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-23 (M2, #234, continuation of #1567):** Reconciled installed CJK viewer fallback with current rendering changes; Windows preserves installed Song/Hei/Kai families and bounds font reads. GBK-EUC-H CIDFontType2 regression coverage includes text extraction; viewer fallback also survives reload with retained preferences. System fonts remain display-only. No overall readiness estimate changed.
- **2026-10-22 (M14, macOS association recovery candidate):** Successful desktop open/save/sign can queue recovery of an existing per-file binding to the running PDFCraft bundle through NSWorkspace. Metadata-only watcher changes retain undo and avoid false overwrite prompts; real same-length/same-mtime edits remain protected. Rebased on 0.12.0 and reuses #3042's shared atomic metadata preservation; the bounded recovery parser does not narrow save limits. Prior native Preview TEST-signature and Finder workflows passed on macOS 27.0.1; other versions remain unqualified. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-13 (M7, free image rotation):** A selected page image has a rotate knob (Shift: 15 degree steps) and `image_edit` a `rotate_by` action; the angle is written as one undoable `cm`. Snapping to page edges while moving is in #1740; resize snapping is not done. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-20 (M9 #79 review, M2, M8):** text extraction reads a marked-content /ActualText that marks no glyph where it paints (paths and images, cut to what is visible, along the direction they are drawn), so words drawn as outlines can be found, copied and exported; OCR and scan field detection still count such a page as having no text of its own. Redaction strips /ActualText, /Alt, /E and /T from inline marked content around paint it clips, as it already did around paint it removed, so a partly redacted outlined word leaves no name in the file. Named-layer recognition also needs the layer form to draw n1 and n3. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M4, initial view):** Window and interface options read indirect boolean ViewerPreferences values. Synthetic regressions cover true/false, direct/indirect preference dictionaries and save/reopen; malformed entries retain false defaults. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M3, #3535):** Escape with a menu open closes the menu and keeps the text selection; the next Escape clears it, as before. One keypress used to do both. A synthetic UI regression checks it. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M3, close prompt keyboard):** The "Save changes?" prompt opens with Save chosen (Enter saves, as before) and the arrow keys and Tab / Shift+Tab move the choice to Cancel or Don't save, shown with a focus ring; Enter or Space press the chosen button and Escape still cancels. Before, Enter always saved whatever was Tab-focused. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-21 (M3, #3808):** Page thumbnails in the Inspector can be resized independently from the document and Organize, with a slider, buttons and `thumbnail-zoom` control option. Selection, page following and reload are covered by regressions. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-22 (M5, comment threads):** Replies join the comment their `/IRT` references, not every comment sharing its `/NM`; PyMuPDF names each page's first comment `fitz-A0`, so all replies used to land under page 1. The Comments header counts comments without their replies, with the number of replies in small text under it (in every catalogue), and a reply's thread line runs from the avatar above it to its own. A reply to a reply shows in its thread in the panel too (it was listed nowhere), an `/IRT` that leads to no comment (itself, a loop, a pop-up, an unlisted object) leaves its annotation a comment of its own instead of hiding it (a status or checkmark that leads nowhere is listed nowhere), and a Replace Text caret isn't counted as a reply. The comment tools refuse an `id` several comments share instead of acting on the first (page 1's), and take `page` + `index` for those; `comment_list` reads a status or checkmark from the annotation it was set on, as the panel does, and reports a reply's own on the reply. UI, engine and automation regressions cover it. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-22 (M2, #3107 / PR #3438):** Inline-image parsing falls back to the existing EI heuristic when missing declared samples, headers or terminators exceed the content-window budget, instead of waiting for an impossible allocation. Exact-length parsing for available samples and ordinary large streamed images is retained. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-21 (M14, Brazilian Portuguese):** Completed the `pt-br` catalog against the current 2,674-key union, including signature validation and accessibility messages; no build or compilation run. Localization remains partial because help and unpredictable engine/OS error details are not translated.
- **2026-10-21 (M14, #3557):** Preferences: the OK button stays below the scrolling settings instead of being the last thing in them, so it is in reach in a short window.

- **2026-10-19 (M11, #2178):** Optimize checks image and soft-mask dimensions and byte counts before decoding; hostile-dimension regressions pass. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-20 (M11, #3507):** Image merging keeps trailing comments, split operands and graphics state across content streams. Synthetic tool tests check saved text, rendered regions and exact image rows.

- **2026-10-17 (M8, gap 6, #2662):** A filled cover over a long line counts when it hides half of any one word, not only 40% of the line's words or box, so a black box over one number in a long line — or an unapplied redaction mark over it — no longer audits clean while the number is still extractable; a sliver under half of every word it touches and under 40% of the line is still not flagged
- **2026-10-13 (M10, OCR review, batch and two engines):** Scan & OCR gains a review step, a second engine and Unicode output. Recognition can clean the raster first (opt-in auto-rotate, deskew, denoise, binarize; word boxes map back to the scan) and read with the built-in ocrs engine, an installed tesseract program run as a fenced external process (a validated location that fails closed instead of falling back to the PATH, allow-listed languages, a private temp directory, timeout and output caps), or both: Automatic asks tesseract when the ocrs reading is weak or of unknown confidence, and a ROVER merge keeps the more confident word (an unscored ocrs word yields only to a tesseract word of 70 or more). The OCR Verify screen (Scan & OCR ▸ Correct recognized text) reviews a recognition before it is applied: confidence-coloured word boxes, corrections, a per-language user dictionary (a plain-text file in the preferences folder, so a word added from any document stays on disk after it closes), Re-OCR of a page or a region, Accept/Reject per page or whole. Pages accepted one by one each apply once; a region re-read moves the review marks of the words it keeps, and a whole-page re-read clears that page's marks (undoable in the panel). Output is searchable (an invisible layer, one undo step) or a NEW editable document of visible text. Words beyond WinAnsi are drawn in Type3 faces from craft-fonts with a ToUnicode map in the editable output (characters no bundled face has appear as '?'); the searchable layer writes them as Type3 fonts with empty character programs and a ToUnicode map, so extraction reads them back with no fonts and no substitutions; redaction removes that layer like any other text (tests in pdfcraft-redact; the end-to-end engine test needs the craft-fonts build input and is skipped without it). Batch runs skip pages that already have text unless forced, and skip whole files that have text only with the opt-in skip-files flag (off by default; Force OCR overrides both); they refuse two inputs of the same file name and existing outputs before reading anything, and report low-confidence pages at the settings' threshold. Headless: ocr_recognize (engine, strategy, preprocessing, region; the editable output goes to an `out` that must not exist yet, and `out` is refused for a searchable run), ocr_words, ocr_review_apply, ocr_dictionary, ocr_status and ocr_recognize_files. All 21 interface catalogs carry the new strings; the Lithuanian ones were added without native review. Reading with every cleaning step off passes the render through untouched, as on main (the 300 dpi normalization runs only when a cleaning step is on: enlarging a 72 dpi render 4x on its own made the models read "due: €250" as "ue: ?250"). Still missing: the deep scan clean-up options (descreen, background and edge-shadow removal), scan images in the editable output, matching the scanned typeface, and recognition quality beyond the tested English set.
- **2026-10-13 (M10, #1757):** Office export keeps each dotted-leader contents entry in one paragraph, with its page number, instead of inferring table cells from its separate text objects.
- **2026-10-22 (M6, calculation scripts):** The Calculate tab and a rename read simplified field notation only from a comment, where Acrobat writes it: a string that spells `BVCALC … EVCALC` is no longer taken for the notation, or rewritten by a rename. A `/` after the condition of `if`, `while`, `for` or `with` starts a regular expression, so a rename no longer rewrites a name inside one, and a quote inside one no longer hides the `getField` calls after it. Both found in review of #3550. A `//` comment ends at `\r`, U+2028 or U+2029 as well as `\n`, so in a script with `\r` line endings the code after one is no longer hidden from a rename, or taken for the notation. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M6, calculation order page):** Prepare a form ▸ Calculation order, a page of its own, lists the calculated fields in the order they calculate with their formulas and values, moved with Up/Down arrows (as Acrobat's Set Field Calculation Order), Alt+↑/↓ or by dragging. A card says what is wrong with the order and holds Fix order; a loop has a card of its own that opens one of its formulas. On the page, each calculated field shows its step, and arrows show what the field in focus reads and what reads it. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M6, calculation order):** The order a form's fields calculate in (`/CO`) is read, checked, fixed and set headlessly with `form_calculation_order`: it says which fields read one calculated later (they show stale values), which read each other in a loop, and which the PDF's order leaves out; Fix order moves only what must move, in one undo step, and the form recalculates. Editing a formula no longer moves its field to the end of the order, and a field the order lists twice calculates once. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M2, #3574):** Forms without Resources use page Properties for ActualText and layer visibility. Synthetic tests cover boundaries, nested/shared forms, and save/reopen. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M2, PR #3348 review):** Tracking samples retain short repeated punctuation (up to four glyphs), keeping tracked ellipses intact and searchable while longer TOC leaders still preserve title word spaces. Synthetic regressions cover isolated and surrounded ellipses. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M2, #3601):** A missing spacing glyph in an OpenType substitute uses its ordinary-space glyph. Synthetic Japanese CID renderer tests check blank spaces, `/W` and `/DW` placement, and unchanged Unicode extraction. Font selection and embedded fonts are unchanged. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M12, #3673):** Character encoding accepts Identity-H/V fonts with Adobe CJK collections and recognizes predefined non-Identity CMaps independently. Unknown CMaps and foreign registries remain refused; synthetic checker and read-only automation regressions passed after failing before the fix. Per-glyph mapping validation and embedded-font extraction remain separate. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-19 (M6, renames keep formulas):** Renaming a field renames it in every calculation that reads it, in the same undo step: `AFSimple_Calculate` field lists, simplified field notation (the comment and the JavaScript after it) and `getField` calls naming it in one string literal; the rest of each script is kept as written. A sum used to drop the renamed field, notation read it as 0 and a script kept a stale value. Field Properties says how many formulas changed and `form_set_props` lists them as `formulas_updated`. Names built at run time and other events' scripts are not rewritten. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-19 (M6, text field table):** Prepare a form ▸ Text field table drags out an area and adds rows and columns of named text fields as one undoable edit, laid out on the page as displayed, with a live preview, per-column name patterns and widths, and refusal before anything is added when a cell would be too small or a name taken; `form_add_field_table` does the same headlessly. `form.multiple-copies` is partial: copying an existing field into a grid is still missing. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-11-16 (M6, copies of a field):** `form_copy_field_grid` copies an existing field of any type but a radio button into a grid of rows by columns as one undoable edit: each copy is a field of its own with the same type, flags, options, look and actions (a check box keeps its on state and starts off, a calculated copy joins the calculation order) and no value, laid out as the page is shown and refused whole when a copy would run off the page or a name clashes. `form.multiple-copies` stays partial: the right-click window with a preview is still missing. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-17 (research):** Added a [PDF workflow research reference](docs/research/pdf-workflows/README.md): dated public-source locators, 20 workflow research domains, a pinned feature mapping and interaction evidence. Reference documentation; no feature statuses, milestone priorities or estimates changed.

- **2026-10-16 (M7, #2516):** A paragraph number from `text_paragraphs` keeps naming the paragraph it listed after edits regroup the page: `text_edit`, `text_edit_layout` and `page_render`'s `hide_text_block` find it again by its text and box, and refuse one that was merged into a neighbour (the edited paragraph included) instead of rewriting the paragraph that took its number (`stale_paragraphs` lists the numbers gone). The inline editor's switch-paragraph click holds the same key. No stable identity across save and reopen yet (#2508).
- **2026-10-19 (M7, #3591):** A background added to a page that already has one is painted over the old one instead of going under it and staying hidden. Behind the content, backgrounds come first, in the order added, and then behind-content watermarks, so a watermark added after a background no longer disappears. Synthetic regressions in `crates/edit` check the order. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-19 (M2, radial luminosity masks):** Added synthetic radial-fade coverage for the existing masked-gradient fix (#2132): centre, midpoint, antialiased clip and per-pixel shading reference, including previews, scales and tiles. Removing the existing native-gradient mask guard reproduces an opaque midpoint; restoring it passes. No new renderer behavior or estimate change.
- **2026-10-20 (M6, #3502):** Form detection preserves comments across content streams and does not add fields over rectangles hidden by those comments. Synthetic tests cover visible controls, graphics state, and saved field inventories. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M6, #2273 / PR #2279):** Rebased the field-highlight default and Appearance preference onto main, retaining secured-form parity evidence and updating secured AcroForm/XFA toggle expectations. Build and test validation delegated to GitHub CI. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M5, reshape lines):** A line or arrow could only be moved after it was drawn; its length and angle stayed. A selected line, connected lines, polygon or cloud now has a handle on each end or corner: dragging one reshapes it (Shift holds it to 45°), as one undo step, with the new shape drawn while dragging. `pdfcraft_annot::set_points`, `Edit::ReshapeAnnotation`, `comment_edit points`; comment lists carry the points. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M3, #3620):** Overlapping comment hover uses the original annotation index independently of spatial inspection order. A synthetic UI regression covers different annotation upper edges. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-20 (M3, #3541/#3620):** Comment hover follows the individual marked lines instead of the enclosing multiline rectangle, and overlapping comments follow paint order. Synthetic UI regressions cover all four text-markup kinds, page/view rotations, neighboring highlights, legacy rectangle fallback and unchanged PDF bytes. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M10, #3532 partial):** Vector printing retains existing widget artwork when its appearance lacks Subtype. A print-owned copy adds Form dispatch metadata. The source stream and all explicit subtypes stay unchanged. Synthetic source/output raster checks cover resources, selected/shared appearances, placement, flags and content modes. Appearance regeneration and physical printer validation remain outside this fix. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M5, several comments):** Comment Properties opened on one of several selected comments, the context menu's Colour and the quick bar's colour change all of them as one undo step, each where the property applies (a font size to text boxes, a fill to shapes, a width to lines and shapes); one that stays locked keeps its look. Copied Fill & Sign ticks, crosses, dots and lines hang on the pointer as themselves rather than as empty boxes, and copied text boxes show their text (`FillMark::strokes` is shared by the appearance and the outline). Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-19 (M5, comments between documents):** Comments copy and paste, within and between documents: ⌘C with comments selected copies them as XFDF (each with its replies) to the app's and the system clipboard, ⌘V / Edit ▸ Paste comments / the page menu pastes them onto the current page as new comments under names of their own (`Edit::PasteComments`, `pdfcraft_xfdf::import_comments` with a target page and fresh names, so nothing is replaced and pasting twice gives two sets; `export_xfdf_comments` writes a selection with its replies). Several comments select at once (⌘-click, ⇧-click, on the page and in the panel); Delete removes them as one step and a drag moves them together. Connected lines, polygons and clouds finish from the right-click menu's Complete (Undo last point, Cancel drawing beside it), as other editors do. Tools `comments_copy` / `comments_paste`. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-20 (M5, arrow keys move comments):** With a comment selected, the arrow keys move it a screen pixel and ⌘/Ctrl or Shift and an arrow ten, instead of scrolling the view or turning the page; up is up on screen at any zoom and rotation, and each press is one undo step. Text markup and locked comments stay put. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-20 (M8, #3647):** Apply prunes fully removed tiling patterns, then checks retained pattern resources for text, images and unreadable content before reporting success. Shared patterns, Form-owned and inherited patterns, and cells kept by another page cause an atomic refusal when safe removal cannot be shown. Synthetic saved-file and automation regressions cover refusal, preserved marks and undo, and vector-only controls. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-20 (M1, PR #1143 review):** Repair-aware dictionary/preflight edits and snapshot restoration preserve both accepted generations of a damaged live slot. Such edits use the existing full-save fallback; classic and packed rewrites coalesce aliases so page-tree parent links retain one object identity. Explicit generation replacement remains single-generation. Materialized xref diagnostics inspect retained primary and hybrid rows before folding, retaining the 256-object cap plus summary and discarding abandoned-index notes. Synthetic red/green controls cover preflight, undo restoration, equal values, explicit narrowing, encryption packing and four save variants. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-19 (M14, #3373):** The main menu and its long submenus scroll in short windows, keeping their commands reachable. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-19 (M3, #3387):** Save-prompt shortcuts yield to later consent and utility dialogs; a synthetic UI regression checks that an edited document stays open. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-18 (M3, PR #3101 review):** Gap changes in Organize Pages preserve document scroll position; menu and control changes apply to both comparison panes without changing unrelated tabs. Three synthetic UI regressions were observed failing before the fixes. Readiness estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-18 (M3, PR #3101):** Optional gapless continuous page display with persisted defaults and UI control support; both new labels translated in all 22 catalogs. Rebased onto current main, preserving navigation and rendering fixes. Readiness estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-18 (M10, #3120):** Export to Word, PowerPoint, HTML and RTF reads text from form XObjects nested as deep as the renderer draws them (50 levels), and up to 50 000 forms a page (the renderer's budget for nested ones); it stopped at 12 levels and 4 096 forms and left the rest out silently. A page past those limits is listed in `doc_export_office`'s `truncated_pages`, with a warning. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-18 (M5, PR #1971 review):** Nested FDF annotation values that identify a source `/Page` or `/Pages` dictionary become null at every copy depth. A synthetic three-page PDF saved and reopened before export verifies that destination actions cannot pull source page content into the FDF, while unrelated nested metadata and shared references remain intact. FDF import semantics, copied-graph limits and estimates are unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M5, PR #1971 refresh):** Nested FDF annotation values retain shared or cyclic copied graphs. Discovery borrows direct values and records annotation positions with lazy array owners; retained entries are budgeted before cloning. Synthetic stress fixtures include repeated aliases and saved-PDF cache eviction. Limits cover the copied graph; source parsing and page discovery retain their existing limits, with no total source-memory bound. FDF import semantics and estimates are unchanged.
- **2026-10-13 (M5, nested FDF values):** Comment export copies and renumbers nested indirect values, retaining geometry, colours, borders and shared graphs. Graph/data limits return an error before the destination is written; synthetic saved-PDF and automation exchange regressions. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M2, #3086):** Text extraction estimates letter tracking without repeated punctuation gaps so dotted TOC leaders do not swallow word spaces. Synthetic PDF regressions cover leader lengths, phrase search and selection; strongly tracked text stays intact. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-18 (M6, #2892):** Push-button normal labels are editable per widget through Options and form_set_props, independently of field names; rendering, undo/redo and incremental/full save/reopen are covered. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-18 (M7, #3212):** Headers, Bates numbers, watermarks and added text stay visible after unfinished marked content, with original content preserved.
- **2026-10-18 (M12, #2589):** Active XFA skips PDF-container fallback scripts while retaining requested messages and saved scripts; ordinary/static/unlaid-out documents and explicit console/field calls keep their existing behavior. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M7, #2592):** Styled fallback fonts batch up to 240 glyphs per actual face and retain source styles. Budget refusal and distinct same-BaseFont descriptor weights have mutation-checked regressions; coverage and complex mixed runs remain limited. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M7, #3187):** Added-image automation normalizes extreme signed rotation angles before combining them with the current orientation; integer bounds, history, pixels, save/reopen and MCP have regressions. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M12, #2569):** Growable multiline XFA text reflows after edits, with undo/redo, save/reopen and print coverage; oversized fields still become read-only page text. Structured-data synchronization warnings retain accepted whitespace without relayout from stale data. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M12, #2572):** Every repeated page-master button resolves its own field to the shared XFA click script, including print buttons. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M12, #2570):** Dynamic XFA rich-text fields carry exData/maxLength into AcroForm MaxLen for reported and enforced Unicode character limits, retained after save/reopen. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-19 (M4, #3309 review):** Page deletion keeps working with 60,000 named destinations, without a name-count traversal limit. Synthetic regressions verify every surviving target after deletion and restoration of the deleted page and its destinations by undo/redo, full/incremental save/reopen and unchanged rendering. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M4, #3309):** Deleting a page prunes named destinations without recursive calls. Cyclic and shared indirect nodes are visited once. Synthetic deep/cyclic graph, save/reopen and automation regressions cover the change. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M1, #2658):** macOS atomic saves retain destination extended attributes, including Finder tags, and reject partial resource-fork reads after size changes. Metadata failures retain the original PDF and unsaved edit. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M7, Edit text caret):** In a text region that mixes sizes (a 24 pt title over 12 pt lines), the inline editor's caret on a small row was as tall as the region's first size and reached into the row below: egui draws the caret at least one row of the text field's font tall, and that font had the paragraph's first size. The field's font is now half the region's smallest size, so each row's own height decides. Kittest regression; checked in the app on a real document. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M3, #2781):** The Pages panel follows the current page, as Acrobat's page thumbnails do: going to a page whose thumbnail is out of sight (scrolling, Page Down, Go to page, a bookmark) scrolls the panel just enough to show it. A thumbnail already in view, or clicked, leaves the panel where it is, and a page change while the user scrolls the panel or drags thumbnails does not move it. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M12, #1645):** CLI info, text and render use the engine's dynamic XFA layout rather than placeholder pages. Synthetic two-page and encrypted-form regressions cover counts, text, pixels, copy permissions and unchanged input bytes. Estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-18 (M4, PR #1919 review):** repeated page indices apply each rotation to the latest page-local value, preserving indirect/inherited angles. Synthetic adjacent/interleaved duplicates retain siblings and page contents through incremental and full saves. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M4, #1919 refresh):** Integrated upstream `99941daa` while retaining the indirect page rotation correction and all upstream progress. Only independent documentation conflicts required resolution; focused production and test files are unchanged from the previously green CI head. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M3, #3149):** Page replacement cancels render demand collected for the previous geometry; all viewer raster upload paths reject empty or oversized textures before cache eviction. Synthetic regressions reproduce the reported 1880×2350 upload at a 2048 limit, retain cached images and unsaved edits, and resume bounded backdrops and tiles. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-19 (M3, #3360):** Switching between Read and the docking workspace retains the current document’s scroll position at fixed zoom, including within-page position; separate tabs retain their own offsets. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M8, #1350):** Integrated current main while preserving content-stream comment boundaries. Hidden-text cleanup retires unused named replacement descriptions on rewritten pages and forms, including inherited/shared resource holders, without removing descriptions still used by surviving content. Synthetic saved-byte and preservation regressions cover the cleanup. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-17 (M4, #2997):** Dragging page thumbnails in the Organize grid with Ctrl held (Option on macOS) drops copies at the gap instead of moving the pages: a "+" badge sits by the pointer, the copies land at the gap selected, and one undo step ("Duplicate pages") takes them out again. `page_duplicate` takes `at` (1-based, where the first copy goes; left out, after the last of them) so agents can drive the same thing, and the UI control channel's `ui.drag` modifiers now reach gestures that read the input state. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M7, Add Background):** A background added behind a page that paints its own opaque white backdrop (browsers' Save as PDF, print drivers) was hidden completely, so the tool looked broken. The dialog now detects such pages and offers to replace the backdrop (on by default): the one fill becomes `n` in a per-page copy of its stream, and Remove background turns the fill back on, after edits and redactions too, unless the operators before it changed or it would no longer be painted first; then it says which pages keep it off. The copy's tag holds no reference to the original stream, which had kept text a redaction or Remove Hidden Information removed in the saved file. Scanned pages are warned about, not touched. New `doc_backdrops` tool; `doc_background` takes `replace_backdrop`. The default colour is a visible light yellow and the preview shows text over it. Acrobat's own behaviour on such pages is unobserved. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M6, AcroForm XML text):** Plain AcroForm XML imports retain all direct text around comments and processing instructions, including CDATA and character references. Synthetic regressions verify exact values and unknown catalog data after incremental and full save/reopen. Nested-element and XFA interpretation are unchanged. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M6, #1917 refresh):** Integrated upstream `99941daa` while retaining the XML/XFDF attribute whitespace correction and all upstream progress. Only independent documentation conflicts required resolution; focused production and test files are unchanged from the previously green CI head. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M1, #2267 refresh):** Integrated upstream `99941daa` while retaining the raw-deflate fallback correction and all upstream progress. Only independent documentation conflicts required resolution; focused production and test files are unchanged from the previously green CI head. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M10, #1201 partial):** Lossless image export applies each component's Decode pair. Indexed values round and clip before palette lookup. Color-key masks keep raw samples. The existing 16-bit precision limit remains. JPEG and mask Decode, ICC transforms, and calibrated colors remain outside this fix. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M3, #2739):** Wrapped revision buttons stay above Binding in Document Properties. A synthetic regression checks control bounds and opens a saved revision at two viewport widths. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-19 (M7, tracked text):** Editable paragraphs and object labels distinguish repeated glyph tracking from word gaps in TJ arrays and separately positioned glyphs. Synthetic regressions cover character spacing, horizontal scaling, rotated baselines, larger word gaps, and rows of one-glyph words or cells (inferred tracking is capped at half an em, so `a b c` and digit or Y/N columns keep their spaces). Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M9, #79):** a signature appearance that follows the named-layer convention (its /N draws one Form that holds and draws n1 and n3) shows the signature's real state: n1, n3 and n4 are redrawn in the display copy with the panel's icons and a caption in the interface language (Helvetica; else shaped in embedded Inter, or in a craft-fonts face for Arabic, Japanese and Telugu; else English, as Chinese always is). Exports, both print modes and the CLI's render show the redrawn state; saves never do. Every signature keeps its badge, since a document can clip or cover its own layers. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M9):** the page validation overlay moves to the widget's bottom-right corner as it lies on the page (so it turns with the page and the view) and takes a quarter of the widget's shorter side, at most 48 screen points, so it annotates the signer's artwork from a corner instead of covering the middle of it; a field too small for a legible mark keeps its outline, a rectangle the screen cannot place is not drawn at all, and the whole widget answers the pointer. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M5, #2669, item 5):** The Comments panel groups replies by name and counts all page roots once per frame. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M7, #2140):** Header & footer (and Bates numbering, which uses the same dialog) can shrink the page content so the header and footer don't cover it: the original content is scaled evenly into the band they leave free, centred across, also on rotated pages; Remove and Update take the scaling away, and margins that leave no room are refused. Dialog checkbox with a preview of the band, `shrink` on `doc_header_footer`, all catalogs. Annotations and added text and images are not moved yet. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-20 (M14, Spanish catalog gaps):** `es.tsv` gains the 143 `(context, English)` keys the other catalogs carried and it was missing, so Spanish covers the whole bundled key union like Lithuanian and Vietnamese: calendar months and weekdays, the `certificate` context, X.509 key usages, signature-revision and digital-ID lines, password validation, eight plural messages, the combine-from-folder strings, the rendering and stale-file notices and the shortcut-spelled tooltips. Counts in `localization-parity.md` re-measured against the current union (`vi` restated against it for the same reason). Status stays `partial`: no translated help, and OS details stay outside the catalog. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M14, localization):** Czech goes from menus-and-dialogs to the whole surface: 2,674 entries after adding the ~220 keys the other catalogs had -- the sentences the engine words (signature details, accessibility findings and report, PDF/A problems, pages to print, history labels), the date picker's weekday heads, the certificate viewer's fields and shared buttons. "Tag" is *tag* throughout (the Tags panel said *značky*, which is also a redaction mark), and "{n} tags" no longer reads "1 značek". Two new catalog tests. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M5, #2583, #1869):** A sticky note on a page with `/Rotate` is drawn upright, with its top-left where it was placed. Notes are saved with the NoRotate flag, and the renderer now honours it: the appearance is turned against the page's rotation about the upper-left corner of `/Rect`, which stays put (ISO 32000-2 §12.5.3; vendored hayro-interpret patch 37). Attachment icons follow the same rule. Selection, hover, the edit composer, the Comments panel's flash and the `comment_list` and `doc_info` rectangles use the drawn box, and `comment_add` anchors an icon's displayed top-left at `at`. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-16 (M11, #1201):** Optimize decodes a JPEG with its colour transform: an Adobe APP14 marker's flag, else the filter's `/ColorTransform` (ISO 32000-2 §7.4.8). An RGB JPEG stored untransformed (`/ColorTransform 0`, no marker) used to be converted from YCbCr anyway and came out in other colours, recompressed or merged from strips; it now keeps them. A JPEG whose marker and `/ColorTransform` disagree, or whose components are named R, G and B without `/ColorTransform 0`, is left as it is, since readers differ on it. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-17 (M10, gap 8):** File ▸ Open opens Word, Excel, PowerPoint, OpenDocument and RTF files as new, unsaved PDFs, converted on the open worker by an installed LibreOffice (headless, a private temporary folder and fresh profile with macros off, stopped after 120 s); without LibreOffice the user is told it is needed. The Open dialog lists PDF (the default), Images, Office documents, Text and All files. Insert, Combine and Create ▸ Multiple files refuse Office documents for now, since they convert on the frame thread. `create.from-office` planned → partial. On 2026-10-26 the profile also stopped Writer and Calc from updating linked content (Impress has no such setting), and tests that skip without LibreOffice convert generated DOCX, XLSX, PPTX and RTF files through `doc_create_multiple` and `doc_combine` and check their text. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M2, gap 12):** `cargo xtask text-oracle` scores reading order as well as word F1: the longest common subsequence of the two word sequences over the longer one. F1 is a bag of words and cannot see order at all -- measuring #2431, a change that moves text and nothing else, it called 26 of the 29 files whose output changed identical, where the order score separated 18 improvements from 6 regressions. Reports, does not gate; a disagreement with Poppler, not a verdict. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M2, #2305 follow-up):** WinAnsiEncoding 0xA0 and 0xAD and MacRomanEncoding 0xCA extract as the ordinary space and hyphen Annex D.2 names them, in the vendored hayro tables (patch 30) as well as `crates/fonts`, so search, copy and redaction see characters that were hidden behind U+00A0 and U+00AD. Across the pdf.js corpus the soft hyphens we extract fall 117 to 13 and the non-breaking spaces 30 to 26; the rest come from `/ToUnicode`. An explicit `/Differences` name keeps its glyph list value, as pdftotext does. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M14, gap 16, #1683):** Split view. View ▸ Split (⌘\\ / Ctrl+\\) and a tab's Split right / Split down show two panes, each with its own tab strip, scroll and zoom; a tab dropped on the right half of the page area splits right, and the divider drags. One document in both panes is one document with one Undo; closing one of its views keeps it open. The pane last clicked is the one menus, keys and panels act on. Nothing about the panes is kept across restarts. `view.split-window` planned → shipped. Multiple windows stay planned. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M12, XFA, #2574):** A `pageArea` with no `<occur>` repeats without limit instead of taking a subform's default of one use, so a later page area with `occur max="1"` is no longer drawn (with a copy of its fields) over every body page. A page area's `occur min` is honoured too: one the body never reached gets the pages it still needs after the body (the once-only closing page of a form, with its button, comes last). Acrobat Pro also puts that page last, and when printing keeps it but leaves it blank (`relevant="-print"` on the page area), which PdfCraft does not do yet; both re-observed on a synthetic fixture, see the 2026-10-19 line. Synthetic regression tests. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-19 (M12, XFA, #2574):** The Acrobat observations behind the line above were first made on a public real-world form, which the Privacy rule does not allow; they are replaced by ones made on a synthetic form (three `breakBefore pageArea` sections, then a page area with `occur min="1" max="1"`, with and without `relevant="-print"`). Acrobat Pro shows the closing page last in both and prints it in both, blank only with `relevant="-print"`: confirmed. The synthetic form also shows a difference not fixed yet: Acrobat opens it as 5 pages, with an empty first body page before the first section, where PdfCraft lays out 4. Two more synthetic forms narrow it down: with `breakBefore targetType="pageArea"` on the first section, with or without `startNew`, Acrobat opens an empty body page first; without a break on the first section it lays out 4, as PdfCraft does (so `startNew` is not the cause). Not fixed yet: `layout.rs` skips a break while the page is still empty. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M10, print a selected area, #2051):** The area Take a Snapshot copies stays outlined with a small menu under it, Print area… and Save as image… (PNG). Print area… opens Print with Selected area (page N, size) under Pages to Print: the area prints alone, as a page of that size, with Fit, Actual size, Shrink or a custom scale, clipped to the area; Multiple, Booklet, Poster and Label stay for pages. `doc_print` takes `area`. New parity entry `print.selected-area` (shipped). Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M7, find and replace in Edit text):** In Edit text mode the floating Find box gains a Replace with row (Edit ▸ Replace…, ⇧⌘H / Ctrl+Shift+H): Replace changes the highlighted match, Replace all every match as one undo step. Each match is rewritten inside its own paragraph through the paragraph rewrite, so fonts, sizes and colours stay and the paragraph rewraps; matches that span paragraphs, sit in OCR text, need another font or lie outside editable paragraphs are left alone and counted. `text_replace` drives the same plan. `edit.find-replace` planned → partial. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M8, #2447):** Removing links or comments (Remove Hidden Information, Sanitize) also drops the tagged-PDF structure tree's object references to them, so the full rewrite no longer keeps a removed link's URL or a comment's text. Regression seen to fail without the fix. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M6, CSV tab-containing headers):** CSV export and spreadsheet merging quote tabs, and import distinguishes quoted tabs from TSV delimiters without changing field identities. Synthetic export/import and incremental/full save regressions cover header tabs, quotes and line breaks, plus ordinary TSV controls. Formula-cell policy is unchanged. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M6, AcroForm XML text):** Plain AcroForm XML imports retain all direct text around comments and processing instructions, including CDATA and character references. Synthetic regressions verify exact values and unknown catalog data after incremental and full save/reopen. Nested-element and XFA interpretation are unchanged. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-13 (M6, data exchange fidelity):** XML and XFDF exports preserve tabs and line breaks in field names and comment metadata using character references. Saved-PDF regressions cover distinct names that XML would normalize to the same attribute value, restored field values and replacement of the original comment identities. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M12, XFA, #2574):** A `pageArea` with no `<occur>` repeats without limit instead of taking a subform's default of one use, so a later page area with `occur max="1"` is no longer drawn (with a copy of its fields) over every body page. Synthetic regression test; the page area's `occur min` (a once-only page of its own) is still not laid out, recorded in `form.xfa-dynamic`. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-14 (M12, reading colours, #2059):** Pending page backgrounds use the document's reading colour immediately, preventing white flashes while scrolling to uncached pages. A headless canvas regression covers three pending pages in every preset and a custom background. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (community, M6, #2608):** In Prepare a form the selected field moves with the arrow keys: a point per press, ten with Shift, as the object nudge and dragging it do. Each press is one undo step ("Change field properties"), and the keys no longer turn the page while a field is selected. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M3, closable community card):** The ArtCraft community card on Home has a close button that hides it for the rest of the run; it returns on the next launch, and its links stay in the top bar's Discord button and the Help menu. A UI regression covers closing and the fresh-launch default. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M14, Combine files from File Explorer, #585):** The Windows MSI adds "Combine files in PdfCraft" to PDF files' right-click menu (with the Edit verb, same switch); each selected file starts `pdfcraft --combine`, and the single-instance hand-off (`"combine": true`, `OsEvent::Combine`) collects them into the running PdfCraft's Combine files tab. Tests: the request round trip and hand-off in `single_instance.rs`, the OS event in `tests/ui.rs`, the verb rows in `test-msi.ps1`. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M14, MSI install folder, #1139):** The Windows installer's welcome dialog has an "Install to" row with a native folder browser, starting on the folder chosen for the per-user or per-machine scope (the recorded one on an update). `test-msi.ps1` checks the dialog wiring: both Install buttons set the folder before the dialog closes, and Browse's OK and Cancel. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M8, #2448):** Applying redactions also removes the document information entries and the document's and pages' XMP metadata that repeat the removed text, so a redacted word can't survive in `/Title`, `/Keywords` or the XMP that mirrors them. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-13 (community, M3, #1569 review):** Pixel-only Wayland wheel, wheel-tilt and continuous input use standalone moved events, so Ctrl zoom does not remain latched after the modifier is released (#991); vendored winit patch (13), with a policy test. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-18 (M1, PR #1143 repair policy):** A live in-file reference resolves when its generation matches the xref row or a same-number header at that indexed offset. Neither-match and free slots remain null, including when old object bytes remain. Opening records bounded xref/header disagreements without parsing object bodies or expanding compact xref rows. Synthetic controls cover read order, clones, overlays, dictionary edits, full classic/packed saves and an independent reader of incremental output. **Correction to the 2026-10-15 note below:** its async-opening-test sentence was not evidenced by this COS change; no fresh async-opening or GUI validation is claimed. The full-reference stream-length recursion key is defensive hardening, not an independently isolated regression fix. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M1, PR #1143 refresh):** COS reference lookups and full saves distinguish complete object references, so stale generations cannot expose replacement content. Snapshot restoration also keeps edits to objects with nonzero generations before incremental saves. Synthetic regressions cover caches, overlays, snapshots, stream lengths and classic/packed saves. Current upstream async-opening test corrections are included. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M14, #2383 candidate):** Device-limit requests now clamp unsupported maxima and minimum alignments before enabling the adapter's full 2D texture extent. This addresses the reported V3D request of 16 shader variables on an adapter supporting 15. Synthetic regressions fail before the clamp and pass afterward, along with the existing HiDPI case and Windows GPU-required application tests. Raspberry Pi startup remains unverified; milestone and overall estimates are unchanged.
- **2026-10-12 (M6):** XFDF field import and data-file extraction join all text nodes in a value, preserving text after XML comments and processing instructions. Synthetic regressions cover Unicode, CDATA, empty values and PDF save/reopen. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-12 (M7, picture right-click menu):** The Edit text & images menu of a picture gains Cut Image, Redact Image (a redaction mark over the picture, applied from Redact a PDF), Create Link… and Recognize Text… (for that page), in all 21 catalogs. The menu was drawn only while the pointer was over the picture, so its items outside the picture (most of a logo's menu, and Delete below a page) could not be clicked; an open menu now stays drawn. Still missing from Acrobat's menu: Arrange (bring to front, send backward), Remove crop, Draw freehand and Edit image using… (opening an external editor).
- **2026-10-15 (M14, Norwegian Bokmål localization):** Added Norwegian Bokmål (`nb`) as an interface and date language: a catalog covering every key the other catalogs have, Norwegian key names, month and weekday names for Fill & Sign dates, and `no`/`nn` locales resolving to it until a Nynorsk catalog exists. Written from the meaning of the English labels; terminology reviewed by a native-speaking contributor, not yet every line. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-12 (M2, inline-image boundaries):** Unfiltered inline images with known sample layouts keep pixel bytes that look like the `EI` terminator when content is rewritten. Regression tests cover row padding, colour-space and parameter aliases, masks and malformed sizes; classic and packed saved PDFs retain the pixels in independent readers and renderers. Filtered and resource-defined layouts retain the existing tolerant heuristic. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-12 (M3, drawing markups):** While a comment is being drawn, the pointer in a band along any edge of the view (or just past it) scrolls the view up, down, left or right, so points beyond what is shown can be reached. A polygon, cloud or run of connected lines takes back its last point with Backspace, Delete or Undo (the first point's removal cancels it). Edge scrolling has unit tests of its maths only; it has not been exercised end to end in the app by an automated test.
- **2026-10-15 (M2, large object streams):** An object stream's index of `objnum offset` pairs is read once per stream instead of once per object fetched from it (vendored hayro-syntax patch 22), so reading every object of a stream of N objects no longer takes N² steps. A Firefox page whose 65,542 objects share one stream (pdf.js corpus `bug1978317.pdf`) drew in 37 s and extracted its text in 40 s; it now takes 0.5 s and 1.9 s, and leaves the robustness baseline's known timeouts. 40,000 pages in one stream open in 0.2 s instead of 54 s. The regression fails before the fix. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-12 (gap 6, #1319, #1305, #1324):** Editing an XFA form keeps a radio item's stored text when it contains a space, so a later layout still selects it. Replace Pages on a split selection replaces those pages and leaves the gap; editing the dialog range still replaces the span. Marking a figure decorative rewrites only that figure's content stream, so a header, added text and unknown stream entries stay. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M14, gap 24):** `misc.localization` and `misc.installers` move from planned to partial. Localization: 22 catalogs ship, but Chinese shows tofu in releases, Telugu has no release font and Arabic is not mirrored. Installers: macOS and Windows releases are signed, Linux packages are not. The Acrobat menu diff itself remains open. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M14, PNG export):** Export ▸ Image as PNG, `pdfcraft-cli render` and the `page_render` automation/MCP tool write an opaque page as RGB at deflate level 3 instead of RGBA at the default level 6: the files stay the same size and are written 3–5× faster (a 300-dpi scan drawn at 220 dpi: 805 → 167 ms; a text page at 220 dpi: 70 → 40 ms; an annual-report page at 150 dpi: 179 → 64 ms). A page with translucent pixels is still written as RGBA. The automation tools use the engine's encoder instead of a copy of it. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (G14, Romanian localization):** Added Romanian (`ro`, Română) as an interface and date language with a 2,673-entry catalog covering every bundled key, and a CLDR plural rule (`plural_ro`: one / few / other, "20 de pagini"). Locale tags `ro` / `ro-RO` / `ro_RO.UTF-8` / `ro-MD` resolve, so Auto picks it on Romanian Windows. Coverage and plural tests added. Status `partial` like the other catalogs (no translated help).
- **2026-10-22 (M14, JPEG and TIFF export):** Export ▸ Image as JPEG or TIFF refuses a raster that is not exactly width × height pixels, as PNG export has since #2425. A raster a pixel short panicked inside the JPEG and TIFF encoders, and a partial pixel at the end was dropped and the rest written as a valid image. Rendered pages are always the right size, so what exports is unchanged. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-13 (G14, Vietnamese localization):** Added Vietnamese (`vi`, Tiếng Việt) as an interface and date language with a 2,591-entry catalog (`plural_none`) covering 100% of the current localizable key union (plus `theme`/`Appearance`). Locale tags `vi` / `vi-VN` / `vi_VN.UTF-8` resolve; calendar months use numeric `Tháng 1`…`12` / `tháng 1`…`12`. Native-speaker community review recorded. The twelve-language localization mean moves from ≈59% to ≈68%; weighted readiness ≈49% → ≈50%. Status stays `partial`: engine/OS details, document contents, filenames, CLI/MCP and external help stay outside the catalog by design.

- **2026-10-12 (M10, Edit on a scanned page):** Turning Edit on (its tab, Edit text & images or Add text) for a scanned page (an image covering at least half of a page with no text of its own; comments and text boxes over it do not count) recognizes the page first, like Acrobat's "Converting scanned page to Editable Text and Images", so its words open for editing. Once per page per window, one undoable "Recognize text" step, with the language of the Recognize Text dialog (English only for now, gap 7). Pages with real text (a flyer with a background picture), read-only files and other tools are left alone. The photographs on a scan are still part of the one page image.

- **2026-10-12 (M2, installed CJK fonts):** A CID font of an Adobe CJK collection (Japan1, GB1, CNS1, Korea1) that a PDF doesn't embed, such as the Kozuka Gothic of an Acrobat text box or the SongtiTC of a Pages export, drew nothing unless craft-fonts had a Japanese face. After craft-fonts, it now draws with a face already installed on the machine (PingFang, Heiti, Microsoft JhengHei / YaHei, Malgun and the like; read at runtime, never bundled, and used only for the on-screen viewer, never for exports, printing or OCR; Noto CJK and Source Han excluded; `PDFCRAFT_SYSTEM_FONTS=0` turns it off). The face of a collection with several regions (PingFang SC, TC) is chosen by region. Checked on macOS with a synthetic Traditional Chinese, Simplified Chinese and Korean page; the Windows and Linux font lists are untested, and a machine with none of the listed faces still draws nothing.
- **2026-10-13 (M3, typed zoom):** The right rail's zoom percentage is an editable field: type 150, 150% or 62.5 % and press Enter, Escape discards, and nonsense keeps the current zoom. The control channel's zoom option shares its parser, which now refuses zero, negative and non-finite values.
- **2026-10-13 (M4, indirect page rotation):** Rotation queries and edits resolve indirect integer values on pages and their page-tree ancestors. Turning a page already at 270° by 90° now saves it at 0°, including when its rotation is inherited. Synthetic full/incremental save-and-reopen tests cover nested inheritance, sibling preservation, negative/large angles and invalid-value fallback. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-14 (M1, Flate fallback):** Raw deflate whose first bytes resemble a zlib header no longer loses a decoding-limit error during fallback. Strict and tolerant filter regressions and a COS stream regression cover refusal at the caller limit and successful decoding within it. No parity status or estimate changes.
- **2026-10-12 (M6, XML field names):** AcroForm XML data export constrains generated element names to XML 1.0 NCNames, so Latin-1 names such as `Area²` and `Aµ` produce readable data files. Original PDF field names travel through the existing `xfdf:original` attribute; previously valid tags, including Greek and CJK, keep their spelling. Saved-PDF regressions cover invalid prefixes, combining letters and normalized-name collisions. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-12 (M6, CSV carriage returns):** Form-data CSV export quotes carriage returns in field names and values, so a trailing carriage return cannot turn into a record separator and disappear on reimport. Saved-PDF round-trip regressions cover CR, LF, CRLF, commas and quotes; estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-13 (community, M14, #1553 review):** Relative Fontconfig includes resolve beside each including config, including nested configuration selected through `FONTCONFIG_PATH`. Directory includes accept only digit-prefixed `*.conf` in lexical order. Isolated regressions reproduce both review findings; configuration size/count/depth limits, cycles, symlinked config and valid reset ordering are covered. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-12 (community, M14, NixOS UI fonts):** Linux/Unix interface fallback fonts are also discovered in Fontconfig's configured directories, including Nix store and user font paths. Existing candidate names, glyph checks, size limits and the screenshot opt-out are retained. Synthetic regressions cover included configuration, symlinks, cycles, depth limits and candidate ordering. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-12 (M7, local text-box candidate):** Add Text opens source text for editing and both text tools retype added items on one click. Draft boxes grow toward the page edge and downwards, with the grown width saved. Single visual rows grow across style boundaries, while multi-row paragraph widths stay fixed. Added-text previews remove only the selected item on a clone, with UI and read-only tool regressions. Arabic shaping, ordering and alignment are outside this candidate. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-12 (M14, #1395):** XDG desktops use the configuration base for app.ron, safely copying existing preferences without overwriting a destination. Original preferences, logs, recovery and digital IDs are retained; failed migration falls back to the original location. Portable and non-XDG locations remain unchanged; estimates unchanged.

- **2026-10-12 (M10, Windows print progress, follow-up to #944):** While a job spools, the progress card counts its sheets ("Sending sheet 2 of 3 to Office…") as craft-print reports them (`submit_with_progress`, storytold/craft-libs#27), and steps aside for another long job's card. Pins craft-libs 666c358. Estimates unchanged.
- **2026-10-15 (M2, renderer speed):** Three vendored hayro-interpret patches (31–33), pixel for pixel the same over the first three pages of every pdf.js corpus file. A sampled (Type 0) function keeps its samples in one flat array instead of a hash map rebuilt every time a shading using it is painted: `bug1721218` (InDesign) draws in 0.49 s (was 1.34 s), and a 2,097,152-sample ramp painted 30 times in under a second (was 38 s). One decode of a JPEG or Flate image serves every size it is drawn at (only JPX decodes for a size), so zooming a 300-dpi scan in and out costs 35–40% less render work. Coons and tensor patch meshes copy their vertices' colours less: mesh-gradient pages draw 10–16% faster (page 8 of #1584's annual report 773 → 696 ms). Estimates unchanged.
